# ============================================================
# EXTREME NIGHTMARE: THE SINGULARITY
# ============================================================
# 2000+ hubs | 10 drones | Designed for maximum suffering
#
# EXTREME CONSTRAINTS FOR 10 DRONES:
#   - 200 serial entry gates, ALL max_drones=1 AND max_link_capacity=1
#   - 300 dead-end branches scattered across 20 layers
#   - 50 closed loop clusters (15 hubs each = 750 hubs)
#   - 100 micro-gates in series, all max_drones=1
#   - 80 priority mirage chains that ALL dead-end
#   - 200 overflow pockets with max_drones=1 and back-loops
#   - 100-lane funnel merging to 1 hub with max_drones=1
#   - 200-hub grinder with back-traps every 5 steps
#   - 150-hub final squeeze where EVERY hub is max_drones=1
#   - Cross-connections creating 500+ false paths
#   - Only 1 true path hidden through the chaos
#
# With only 10 drones, every bottleneck becomes 10x worse.
# The algorithm must find the ONE viable path through 2000+ hubs.
# ============================================================

nb_drones: 10
